# --- dirty_zone_events ---
add_namespace = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone

# Published when the first post-reveal state transfer proves that the sealed
# territory is no longer only a map overlay: administrations and borders are
# now materialising in the Dirty Zone itself.
news_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone.1
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone.1.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_adiscord_vorkerland_northern_settlement
	major = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	option = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone.1.a }
}

# --- claimant_events ---
add_namespace = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# WKR: arguments inside the worker front
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.1
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	timeout_days = 14
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.1.a
		trigger = {
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			WKR_mandate_open = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
			}
		}
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -1.00 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_chairman_choice_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.1.b
		trigger = {
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			WKR_mandate_open = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 55
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
			}
		}
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 1.00 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_executive_choice_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.1.c
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				WKR_mandate_open = no
				WKR_administration_active = no
			}
		}
	}
}

		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.4.nikita.b

			WKR_presidential_course = yes
		}

		add_command_power = 5
		army_experience = 5

			WKR_executive_course = yes
		}

		add_command_power = 5
		add_political_power = 5

		trigger = {
			WKR_executive_course = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 5000 } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 500 producer = WKR }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_reserve_authority
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.5
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.5.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.5.nikita.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	trigger = {
		WKR_administration_active = yes
		WKR_mandate_open = yes
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
		WKR_chairman_has_majority = yes
		WKR_presidential_course = no
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				WKR_mandate_open = yes
				WKR_chairman_has_majority = yes
				WKR_presidential_course = no
			}
			WKR_appoint_chairman = yes
		}
	}
	option = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.5.a }
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.joint.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collegial_staff_formed }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.vlad.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collegial_staff_formed }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.staff.d
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { tag = VAD }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.staff.a
		trigger = { VAD_collegial_government = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.joint.a
		trigger = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council ruling_only = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_stability < 0.50 } }
		add_stability = 0.01
		add_political_power = 10
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_joint_civil_guarantees
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.joint.b
		trigger = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council ruling_only = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_war = yes } }
		add_command_power = 5
		army_experience = 5
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_joint_staff_primacy
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.vlad.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collegial_staff_formed }
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev ruling_only = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_stability < 0.50 } }
		add_stability = 0.01
		add_political_power = 5
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_crown_warrants
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.13.vlad.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collegial_staff_formed }
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev ruling_only = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_war = yes } }
		add_command_power = 5
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 500 producer = VAD }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_chancery_command
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.14
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.14.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.14.joint.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed } }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.14.vlad.d
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = VAD
		has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_vad_solar_settlement_defined
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.14.vlad.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev ruling_only = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_political_power = 20
		add_war_support = 0.01
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_restoration_mandate
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sol
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_offer_vad_sol_alliance = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sra
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_csl
				}
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_vad_solar_intervention = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solar_intervention = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.14.joint.a
		trigger = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council ruling_only = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_political_power = 15
		add_stability = 0.01
		add_command_power = 5
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_compact_mandate
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sol
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_offer_vad_sol_alliance = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sra
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_csl
				}
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_vad_solar_intervention = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solar_intervention = yes
		}
	}
}

	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15.d

	}

		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.24.c
		ai_chance = { base = 30 modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_equipment = { support_equipment < 150 } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 50 producer = TVA }
		add_war_support = 0.01
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_iteration_production_variance
	}
}

# --- collapse_events ---
add_namespace = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.1
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_not_started = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_close_prewar = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_balance_snapshot = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_collapse = yes
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started
		# begin_collapse refreshes Vorkerland trees before this flag exists.
		# Shabrat's collapse-gated focuses read the flag, so dirty STP after it.
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = STP }
			STP = { mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_expire_precollapse_itoran_armistice = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_resolve_fate = yes
		# State 40 must already belong to WKR. A WRK-scoped actor created
		# before the partition is dropped when this event annexes WRK.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_initial_map = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_claimant_characters = yes
		# change_tag_from transfers only human control. The preference event does
		# not alter the split, Worker's random fate, or any AI-controlled claimant.
		# Old queued collapse.1 events carry no preference and therefore keep WKR.
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker
						AND = {
							NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker }
							NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint }
							NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian }
						}
						AND = {
							NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker }
							has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint
							has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian
						}
					}
				}
				WKR = { change_tag_from = WRK }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian }
				}
				VAD = { change_tag_from = WRK }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint }
				}
				TVA = { change_tag_from = WRK }
			}
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian
		WKR = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_initialize_measured_republic = yes
			inherit_technology = WRK
			load_focus_tree = {
				tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_focus
				copy_completed_from = WRK
			}
			load_focus_tree = {
				tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_focus
				keep_completed = yes
			}
			annex_country = { target = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation transfer_troops = yes }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_relocate_legacy_armies = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_teardown_confederation = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_align_wtd_with_worx = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_claimant_cosmetics = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_all_party_identities = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_identities = yes
			focus_unlock = yes
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.41 days = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.2 days = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					RUS = { exists = yes }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_reveal_v2_scheduled }
				}
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_reveal_v2_scheduled
				RUS = {
					country_event = {
						id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.85
						days = 1095
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.3
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_destruction_resolved
	}

	immediate = {
		# ROOT is the human country. Nested scoped goto from WKR does not
		# move that camera.
		goto_province = 16428
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.4 hours = 1 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.4
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_destruction_resolved
	}

	immediate = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_animate_unity_tower_destruction = yes
		launch_nuke = {
			province = 16428
			use_nuke = no
		}
		40 = {
			damage_building = {
				type = infrastructure
				damage = 0.1
			}
		}
		# Worker's fate is already fixed by the collapse roll, but the public
		# report belongs to the aftermath. Publish it one day after the actual
		# detonation so the story can never announce a rescue before the blast.
		WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.6 days = 1 } }

		# The half-speed tower clip runs for about 24 seconds; leave the
		# temporary actor alive long enough for it to reach its ruins state.
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.5 days = 30 }
	}
}

# Worker's public fate report deliberately lags the physical detonation by one
# day. The four fate flags are rolled during collapse.1; only presentation waits.

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_destruction_resolved

	}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.2
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_not_started = yes
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialized_verified
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialized = yes
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_assertion_started_v2
	}

	immediate = {
		# The first teardown runs in the same tick as the partition. Repeat it one
		# day later, after released-country faction membership has entered the cache.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_teardown_confederation = yes
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
		if = { limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } } VAL = { VAL_open_refugee_waves = yes } }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_central_attrition = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_settle_reserve_caches = yes
		WKR = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_legitimacy = yes
			WKR_initialize_administration = yes
		}
		VAD = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_legitimacy = yes
			VAD_initialize_administration = yes
		}
		TVA = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_legitimacy = yes
			TVA_initialize_administration = yes
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_settle_assets = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_campaign_state = yes
		ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_schedule = yes
		WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_bootstrap_ai_force_designs = yes }
		VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_bootstrap_ai_force_designs = yes }
		TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_bootstrap_ai_force_designs = yes }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_roll_central_initiative = yes
		# The claimant spirits are asserted at the actual war-outbreak stage,
		# after the initial partition and political cache rebuild. This prevents
		# WRK's three obsolete starting spirits from resurfacing in live play.
		if = {
			limit = { WKR = { exists = yes } }
			WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_spirit_progression = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { VAD = { exists = yes } }
			VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_spirit_progression = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { TVA = { exists = yes } }
			TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_spirit_progression = yes }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_spirit_progression_v3
		if = {
			limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } }
			VAL = { VAL_handle_vorkerland_war_outbreak = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { WKR = { exists = yes } }
			WKR = {
				country_event = {
					id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.31
					days = 21
					random_days = 14
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.30
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = IVN
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started

		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved }
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active }
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_mission_activation_v3 }
	}
	immediate = {

		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_mission_activation_pending_v3
	}
}

# A short mobilisation pause prevents the successor states from declaring war
# on the day the map is rebuilt. All later wars still come from visible/AI
# decisions and require a live common border.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.31
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = { tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation tag = WKR }
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claim_wars_authorized }
	}
	immediate = {
		# Last guard before any AI can select a war decision. At this point every
		# successor has existed for several weeks, so faction removal is stable.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_teardown_confederation = yes
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claim_wars_authorized
		# The central district bracket does not depend on a claimant or on a
		# theatre-anchor decision. It opens from the same authorisation so that
		# the nine districts are already fighting one another when the first
		# claimant reaches their border.
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.90 days = 1 }
	}
}

# Central district bracket launch. One day after the war authorisation, so that
# the repeated confederation teardown has reached the diplomatic cache.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.90
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = { tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation tag = WKR }
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claim_wars_authorized
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_stage_central }
	}
	immediate = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_local_brackets = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.92 days = 1 }
	}
}

	}
}

# Shared bounded postcondition for whichever bracket stage was launched last.

		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }

}

	}
}

		# tell Kefreyt immediately instead of leaving the loss as a silent map change.
		VAL = {

	}

# Each second country is materialised on a separate date. HOI4 1.19 rebuilds
# state ownership, supply and front data asynchronously after a tag appears;
# creating two closed-zone countries in one effect can race that rebuild.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.17
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = RUS
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_opened
		NOT = { country_exists = MLR }
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_wave_1

# PWR is freed after the initial partition, so its political cache receives one
# deferred assertion just like the two Ivanland clients. Bounded verification
# may call the same assertion again without changing its scope.

	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.44

	is_triggered_only = yes

		tag = PWR
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_government = technocracy }
			NOT = {
				has_country_leader = {
					character = PWR_Alexey_Lange
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_pwr_technocrat = yes }
}

# Independent watchdog for the selectable ROM mission. Clausewitz can remove a
# selectable mission from the UI without reliably executing its timeout effect;
# this bounded event guarantees that the global lifecycle reaches one outcome.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.45
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = ROM }
	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_watchdog_scheduled
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_active
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_rom_northern_intervention_timeout = yes
		}
	}
}

# Event-driven lifecycle assertion and bounded diplomatic-cache cleanup. It is
# scheduled by mission start, peace/capitulation hooks, startup repair and both
# outcomes. Immediate cleanup plus at most two delayed retries is finite.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.46
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = ROM }
	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_check_pending
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_pending }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_rom_northern_intervention_wars = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_war_with = ZAO
						has_war_with = WPA
						has_war_with = WPS
						has_war_with = PWR
						has_war_with = PSD
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_retry } }
					set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_retry
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.46 days = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_pending
					clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_retry
					log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Frealor northern intervention cleanup exhausted its bounded diplomatic-cache retry."
				}
			}
			else = {
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_pending
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_retry
			}
		}
		else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_check_rom_northern_intervention = yes }
	}
}

# Faction and capitulation caches settle after the foreign war is closed. The

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.43

		OR = { tag = IVN tag = PWR tag = PSD tag = ZAO tag = WPA tag = WPS }
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_restored }
	}

		ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_northern_wars = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.74 days = 3 }
	}

# One final delayed declaration pass handles diplomatic caches that rejected the
# first attempt. Verification waits one more day and never observes this tick.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.74
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = { tag = IVN tag = PWR tag = PSD tag = ZAO tag = WPA tag = WPS }

		ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_surviving_northern_war_fallback = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.78 days = 1 }
	}
}

# White peace must settle before PWR creates the temporary defense faction.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.77
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = IVN
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved }
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = PWR
				PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_cache_retry
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_ivanland_intervention_front = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = PWR
				PWR = { is_subject = no has_capitulated = yes }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_cache_retry }
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_cache_retry
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.77 days = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_cache_retry
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_launch_pending
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failure = yes
		}
	}
}

# The declaration and defender joins above share one Clausewitz tick. Observe
# them one day later and perform at most one war retry plus one join retry. The
# state-controller postcondition also handles a northern victor inheriting the
# original PSD objectives during Ivanland's 120-day preparation.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.82
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = IVN
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved }
	}
	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_launch_pending
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verification_pending
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_front_verified = yes }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_war_retry
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_join_retry
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verified_v1
			log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Ivanland intervention front verified: PWR war and every settlement-state controller are reachable."
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { country_exists = PWR }
					PWR = { is_subject = yes }
					PWR = { has_capitulated = yes }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failure = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_war_retry } }
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_war_retry
				declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = take_state_focus generator = { 90 91 } }
				set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verification_pending days = 3 }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.82 days = 1 }
			}
			else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failure = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_join_retry } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_join_retry
			ZAO = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						NOT = { has_war_with = IVN }
					}
					add_to_war = {
						targeted_alliance = PWR
						enemy = IVN
						hostility_reason = asked_to_join
					}
				}
			}
			WPA = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						NOT = { has_war_with = IVN }
					}
					add_to_war = {
						targeted_alliance = PWR
						enemy = IVN
						hostility_reason = asked_to_join
					}
				}
			}
			WPS = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						NOT = { has_war_with = IVN }
					}
					add_to_war = {
						targeted_alliance = PWR
						enemy = IVN
						hostility_reason = asked_to_join
					}
				}
			}
			PSD = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						NOT = { has_war_with = IVN }
					}
					add_to_war = {
						targeted_alliance = PWR
						enemy = IVN
						hostility_reason = asked_to_join
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verification_pending days = 3 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.82 days = 1 }
		}
		else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failure = yes }
	}
}

# A one-day diplomatic-cache barrier for the initial peripheral war graph.
# The phase controller has already detached every independent participant; this
# event creates the seventeen rivalries and hands their verification back to it.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.63
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { OR = { tag = WKR tag = ZAO tag = VLA tag = ROM tag = SOL tag = TRU } }
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_regional_fronts_after_detach = yes
		}
		else = { clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.3 days = 1 }
	}
}

# One bounded verification retry. The phase controller owns the success/failure
# flags; this event only waits out the second diplomatic-cache tick.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.64
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { OR = { tag = RUS tag = IVN } }
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_regional_war_launch = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_degraded }
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.3 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
		}
	}
}

# The first pass runs only after the native peace cache has advanced. A second
# event is queued before the annex attempt; it disappears with the defeated tag
# on success and becomes the single bounded retry if the engine kept a puppet.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.65
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_pending
		OR = { tag = EYR tag = EGC tag = RIV tag = REV tag = YOR tag = NDN tag = SWB tag = VHV tag = OSV }
	}
	immediate = {
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.66 days = 1 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_central_minor_settlement = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.66
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_pending
		OR = { tag = EYR tag = EGC tag = RIV tag = REV tag = YOR tag = NDN tag = SWB tag = VHV tag = OSV }
	}
	immediate = {
		# The second pass is also the terminal recovery pass. Move the shell's
		# remaining states explicitly before the final annex attempt, so a puppet
		# or capitulated remnant cannot strand district integration forever.
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_force_transfer
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_central_minor_settlement = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_pending
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_force_transfer
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_winner_wkr
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_winner_vad
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_winner_tva
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_winner_wrk
			log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Central minor settlement survived its bounded annex retry."
		}
	}
}

# Living-winner controller for capitulated zero-state district shells. It
# cooperates with the loser-scoped .65/.66 events and never polls.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.69
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA tag = WRK }
		is_subject = no
		NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
	}
	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_controller_scheduled
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_controller_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_sweep_pending_central_minor_settlements = yes
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_matching_pending_central_minor_settlement = yes }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.70 days = 1 }
		}
		else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_schedule_final_showdown = yes }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.70
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA tag = WRK }
		is_subject = no
		NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
	}
	immediate = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_sweep_pending_central_minor_settlements = yes
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_matching_pending_central_minor_settlement = yes }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_settlement_controller_failed
			log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Living winner could not dissolve a pending central district shell after one retry."
		}
		else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_schedule_final_showdown = yes }
	}
}

# TVA's Oitfort protectorate enters the WKR war one day after the edge is
# verified, without permanent polling.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.47
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = WTD
		is_subject_of = TVA
		NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		TVA = { exists = yes has_war_with = WKR }
		NOT = { has_war_with = WKR }
	}

	immediate = {
		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = TVA
			enemy = WKR
			hostility_reason = asked_to_join
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.67 days = 1 }
	}
}

# Postcondition and one bounded retry for WTD joining TVA's live war.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.67
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = WTD
		is_subject_of = TVA
		NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = WKR }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_retry
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_fighting_for_worx
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				TVA = { exists = yes has_war_with = WKR }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_retry }
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_retry
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = TVA
				enemy = WKR
				hostility_reason = asked_to_join
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.67 days = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_retry
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_failed
			log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] TVA subject WTD failed to join the verified TVA-WKR war after one retry."
		}
	}
}

# ZAO's subject cache settles after the initial partition. This bounded
# assertion restores both the authored administrator and the regional wartime
# programme if either was lost during the same-tick confederation teardown.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.68
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = ZAO
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started
	}
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_zao_startup_repair_v1_applied
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_government = pragmatism }
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							character = WRK_Bassam_Zogby
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_zao_administrator = yes
		}
		load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_zao_focus keep_completed = yes }
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.1
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_record_regional_diplomacy_outcomes = yes }
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.2
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.2.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.2.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		VAD = { exists = yes has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_pending }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.2.a
		trigger = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sol
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			# An open invitation cannot overturn a subsequently accepted coalition.
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
					is_in_faction = yes
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAD }
					faction_leader = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes }
				}
			}
			VAD = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_pending
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 85 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_allied_with_vad
		VAD = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes
			puppet = SOL
			set_autonomy = {
				target = SOL
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
				freedom_level = 0.10
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = no }
				create_faction_from_template = {
					template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
					name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_restoration_alliance
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = SOL
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_pending
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_dispatch_guard
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_resolved
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_alliance_accepted
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
		# If the three claimant wars are already verified, join only VAD's live
		# edges. Before that point the normal phase verifier performs the same call.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_regional_allies_to_showdown = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.2.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 15 }
		VAD = {
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_pending
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_dispatch_guard
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_resolved
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_alliance_declined
	}
}

	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.3
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.3.t

		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_accepted
		# A late answer reconciles Volnograd with WKR's surviving showdown fronts.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_regional_allies_to_showdown = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.3.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
		WKR = {
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_invitation_pending
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_invitation_dispatch_guard
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_invitation_resolved
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_declined
	}

# A dated receipt, not a country tag or old callback, owns this deadline.

		}
	}

# --- phase_events ---
add_namespace = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.1
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	timeout_days = 14


	trigger = {
		tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_not_started = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.1.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_side_committees_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_worker_fate_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 45 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.1.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_side_staff_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_worker_fate_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.1.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_side_engineers_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_worker_fate_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_worker
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_joint
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preference_utilitarian
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
}

# Exact country-leader state lives in a separate daily cache. It is repaired
# twice for presentation correctness, but a cache miss never freezes the war.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.14
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = WKR
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
		NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identities_verified_v2 }
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_check_pending_v2
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_identities = yes
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identities_materialized = yes }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identities_verified_v2
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_retry_v2
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_degraded_v2
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_retry_v2 } }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_retry_v2
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_check_pending_v2
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.14 days = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_degraded_v2
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_log_claimant_identity_mismatches = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.4
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_preparation_expired
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_showdown_edge_queue = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.5
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_advance_showdown_launch = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.6
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_reunification }

	immediate = {
		# Liberation or renewed hostilities during the delay must reopen the
		# live campaign instead of discarding its only queued handoff.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_victory_ready = yes } }
			WKR = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
			VAD = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
			TVA = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_central_showdown = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				WKR = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				OR = {
					WKR = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_marked_central_unifier = no
				}
			}
			WKR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_unify_under_wkr = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				VAD = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				OR = {
					VAD = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_marked_central_unifier = no
				}
			}
			VAD = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_unify_under_vad = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				TVA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				OR = {
					TVA = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_marked_central_unifier = no
				}
			}
			TVA = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_unify_under_tva = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.7
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_reunification
	}

	immediate = {
		# A human wartime administration follows the united state only when
		# the unified country has no human controller. Queue settlement before
		# changing control.
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
				WTD = {
					exists = yes
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.7 hours = 1 }
			change_tag_from = WTD
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
				EGC = {
					exists = yes
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.7 hours = 1 }
			change_tag_from = EGC
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
				RIV = {
					exists = yes
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.7 hours = 1 }
			change_tag_from = RIV
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
				YOR = {
					exists = yes
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.7 hours = 1 }
			change_tag_from = YOR
		}
		else = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_finish_delayed_unification = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_reunification = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_offer_post_reunification = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified = yes }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_retry }
				}
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_retry
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.7 days = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified = yes } }
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_failed
				log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Vorkerland unification failed its postconditions after one retry."
			}
		}
	}
}

# Fired in the already-normalized winning claimant's scope when the first rival
# claimant capitulates. Neither choice owns peace processing or the map.

	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA tag = WRK }
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capitulated
		NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_command_choice_resolved }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5.a
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_rival_headquarters_integrated
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_command_choice_resolved
		add_command_power = 10
		army_experience = 5
		add_war_support = 0.01
		ai_chance = { factor = 55 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5.b
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_rival_apparatus_dissolved
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_command_choice_resolved
		add_political_power = 25
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_war_support = -0.01
		ai_chance = { factor = 45 }
	}
}

# check_variable title/desc blocks painted the generic fallback: the GUI
# scope that draws a major news item does not resolve var = global.X.
# Each centre is fire_only_once; the objectives_taken array and the story
# reported-array still own the once-per-campaign guard.
# Doctrine declarations are regional news: only Vorkerland's claimants and
# combatants see them. Five declarations, five events. A single major news
# event with check_variable title/desc blocks silently falls through to the generic
# fallback in this engine: the GUI scope that paints a major news item does
# not resolve var = global.X. Worker's fate already uses one event per
# outcome for the same reason. Each of these is fire_only_once; the
# dispatching effect also owns a per-variant global flag so a duplicated
# call cannot re-fire the news.
news_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.31
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.31.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.31.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	major = yes
	show_major = {
		OR = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_minor_combatant = yes
		}
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	option = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.31.a }
}

# --- postwar_expansion_events ---
add_namespace = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar

# A single next-day verification releases campaign-only status if native
# declaration or coalition entry failed. There is no periodic retry loop.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.1
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_close_external = yes }
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.2
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_settlement_scheduled }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_settlement_scheduled
			EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_create_subject = yes }
			PIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_create_subject = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_sovereign = yes
					OR = {
						EBA = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
						PIV = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.3
					hours = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.3
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.3.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.3.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.3.a }
}

# A joint victory always ends in Worcker's coup. The callback runs after the
# reunification has committed its global flags, so the cabinet guard can pass.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.4
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_establish_world_empire = yes }
}

news_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.5
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.5.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.5.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_vorkerland_world_empire
	major = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	option = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.5.a }
}

# The Unity Tower completes the settlement. Load the next tree in a fresh
# event scope, when the completed focus can be used as a transition condition.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.6
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker }
			load_focus_tree = {
				tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_confederation_focus
				keep_completed = no
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian }
			load_focus_tree = {
				tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_technical_directorate_focus
				keep_completed = no
			}
			WRK_worx_initialize = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_petrichev_empire_can_form = yes }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_establish_petrichev_empire = yes
		}
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
}

news_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.7
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.7.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.7.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	major = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.7.a }
}

# --- preparation_events ---

add_namespace = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.1
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	timeout_days = 14
	trigger = {
		tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar
		NOT = { has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.1.worker
		ai_chance = { factor = 45 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar
					NOT = { has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron }
				}
				set_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
					value = 1
				}
				40 = {
					set_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
						value = 1
					}
				}
				WRK_unrest_initialize = yes
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.63 days = 30 }
				mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron_worker_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.1.army
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar
					NOT = { has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron }
				}
				set_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
					value = 2
				}
				40 = {
					set_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
						value = 2
					}
				}
				WRK_intrigue_initialize = yes
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.63 days = 30 }
				mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron_army_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.1.technical
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar
					NOT = { has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron }
				}
				set_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
					value = 3
				}
				40 = {
					set_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
						value = 3
					}
				}
				WRK_cells_initialize = yes
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.63 days = 30 }
				mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron_technical_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron_tt
	}
}

# A district of the capital has risen. The consequences were applied when
# the riot began; this window only reports them.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.60
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.60.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.60.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.60.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_unrest_riot_report_tt
	}
}

# The committees have found a cell of the network. The consequences were
# applied at the exposure; this window only reports them.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.61
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.61.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.61.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.61.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_cell_exposure_report_tt
	}
}

# Ledger of the chosen apparatus, every 30 days from its choice until the
# prewar phase closes. Each effect guards its own apparatus, so a month in
# which the preparation is suspended passes without a tick and the chain goes
# on; it ends only when the phase flag is gone at the collapse.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.63
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar }
	immediate = {
		WRK_unrest_monthly = yes
		WRK_cells_monthly = yes
		WRK_intrigue_monthly = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.63 days = 30 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { tag = EYR }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20.eyr.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { tag = EGC }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20.egc.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { tag = RIV }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20.riv.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { tag = YOR }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20.yor.d
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	timeout_days = 14
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20.close
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.20.accept
		trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_valid = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 75 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_tt
		hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_accept_offer = yes }
	}
}

# Consent and payment are settled by preparation.20 before this bounded dispatch.

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.22
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_signed
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				is_in_faction = no
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
				FROM = {
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_diplomacy_open = yes
					NOT = { tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
							value = 1
							compare = equals
						}
						FROM = { tag = WKR }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
							value = 2
							compare = equals
						}
						FROM = { tag = VAD }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
							value = 3
							compare = equals
						}
						FROM = { tag = TVA }
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_coalition_of_FROM = yes
		}
	}
}

# Prewar crisis dispatcher: one unseen crisis every 21-42 days while the
# confederation still stands. Each crisis records itself when it opens; the
# chain ends with the prewar phase or once every crisis has been seen.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.40
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_41
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_42
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_43
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_44
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_45
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_46
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_47
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_48
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_41 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.41 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_42 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.42 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_43 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.43 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_44 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.44 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_45 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.45 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_46 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.46 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_47 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.47 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_48 }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.48 }
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.40 days = 21 random_days = 21 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.41
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.41.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.41.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_administrative_office
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_41 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.41.a
		add_stability = -0.03
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_bop
			value = -0.10
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees_add_5 = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.41.b
		trigger = { NOT = { has_political_power < 25 } }
		add_political_power = -25
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_bop
			value = 0.10
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

# Directorate records, experimental milestones and personal scenes.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.31
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.31.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.31.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_factory_workshop
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { WRK_worx_government = yes }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.31.a
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.31.b
		trigger = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		if = {
			limit = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
			add_stability = 0.02
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_worx_story_madness_plus_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = {
					var = WRK_worx_madness
					value = 5
				}
				WRK_worx_refresh = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.31.c
		trigger = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		if = {
			limit = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
			add_stability = -0.01
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_worx_story_madness_minus_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = {
					var = WRK_worx_madness
					value = -5
				}
				WRK_worx_refresh = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.50
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.50.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.50.council
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WRK_worx_supervised }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.50.quiet
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_worx_supervised }
			check_variable = {
				var = WRK_worx_madness
				value = 75
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.50.crisis
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_worx_supervised }
			check_variable = {
				var = WRK_worx_madness
				value = 75
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_factory_workshop
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { WRK_worx_government = yes }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.50.a
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

# --- world_empire_events ---
# The generals' plot of the Shadow of Petrishchev. The balance is read again
# when the event fires: a court that recovered in the meantime meets an empty
# conspiracy.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60.d
		trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_conspiracy = yes }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60.d_faded
		trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_conspiracy = no }
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_headquarters
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WRK_empire_coup_pending }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60.a
		trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_conspiracy = yes }
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop
			value = -0.40
		}
		add_stability = -0.10
		add_war_support = -0.05
		army_experience = -25
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60.b
		trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_conspiracy = yes }
		add_political_power = -150
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop
			value = -0.15
		}
		effect_tooltip = { add_ideas = WRK_empire_delta_generals_seats }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WRK_empire_attack value = 0.05 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_refresh_army = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60.c
		trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_conspiracy = no }
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

# --- WKR siege regime: chairman's whims ---
# Demands .1-.5 fire once each. The focus queues the parade; every answer queues
# the next demand through WKR_siege_schedule_next_whim. A demand that arrives
# after the administration ended or the mandate closed the queue does not fire,
# which also ends the chain.

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.1
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_mountain_militia_assembly
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }
	trigger = { WKR_siege_whim_can_fire = yes }
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_parade_done }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.1.a
		add_war_support = 0.03
		add_political_power = 20
		add_command_power = -15
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -0.05 }
		}
		WKR_siege_count_indulged = yes
		hidden_effect = { WKR_siege_schedule_next_whim = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_war_support < 0.40
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_completed_focus = WKR_siege_revolutionary_tribunals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.1.b
		add_stability = 0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.05 }
		}
		WKR_siege_count_restrained = yes
		hidden_effect = { WKR_siege_schedule_next_whim = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_stability < 0.35
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_completed_focus = WKR_siege_right_of_recall
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_srm_army_org_factor value = 0.03 }
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_srm_army_org_regain value = 0.05 }
			WKR_srm_refresh = yes
		}
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -0.05 }
	}

		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = WKR_srm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = WKR_srm_w2_initiative_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_srm_planning_speed value = 0.10 }
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_srm_breakthrough_factor value = 0.03 }
			WKR_srm_refresh = yes
		}
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.05 }
		add_stability = 0.01
	}
}

# The partner's answer. Counters let several answers arrive in one week; each
# copy consumes one answer in the same order as the description triggers.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_volunteers > 0 }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65.d_volunteers
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_volunteers > 0 } }
			check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_levy > 0 }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65.d_levy
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_volunteers > 0 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_levy > 0 } }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65.d_refused
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_field_switchboard
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65.a
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_volunteers > 0 }
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_front_answer_volunteers value = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_levy > 0 }
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_front_answer_levy value = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { WKR_front_answer_refused > 0 }
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_front_answer_refused value = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		else = {

# --- executive_ledger_events ---
# Namespace ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw: Anton Bagley's civil-war executive.

# The statute session (WKR_ledger_executive_statute): whose signature makes law.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.1
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_ledger_handover
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 25
		add_command_power = 10
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -0.05 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_stability < 0.40
			}
		}
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.05 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.1.c
		ai_chance = { base = 15 }
		add_political_power = -10
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_5 = yes
	}
}

# A directorate plenipotentiary against the Kairholm depot council.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.2
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.2.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.2.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_rail_yard_waybills
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.2.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 3000 }
			}
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1500
			producer = WKR
		}
		add_stability = -0.02
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -0.05 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.2.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 35
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_stability < 0.40
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -15
		add_stability = 0.02
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_4 = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.2.c
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
		add_command_power = -5
		add_manpower = 1000
		army_experience = 10
	}
}

		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed } }

				has_war_with = VAD

		}

	}

			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_stability < 0.40
			}
		}
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.05 }
	}
}

# Outcome of the rescue operation. WKR_ledger_resolve_rescue sets exactly one
# flag before dispatch; each option belongs to one outcome and clears its flag.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_archive }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5.archive.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_refused }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5.refused.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_failed }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5.failed.d
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_perimeter_checkpoint
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5.a
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_archive }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		WKR_ledger_legitimacy_up_6 = yes
		WKR_ledger_vad_legitimacy_down_4 = yes
		army_experience = 15
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_archive }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5.b
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_refused }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.15 }
		WKR_ledger_legitimacy_up_3 = yes
		add_war_support = -0.02
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_refused }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5.c
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_failed }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_stability = -0.03
		add_war_support = -0.02
		add_manpower = -500
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_failed }
	}
}

# Report of a search commission tour. WKR_ledger_resolve_search_tour sets one
# finding flag before dispatch; the matching option clears it.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_survivors }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8.survivors.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_papers }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8.papers.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_false_trail }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8.false.d
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_signal_archive_basement
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8.a
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_survivors }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_manpower = 1000
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_4 = yes
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_survivors }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8.b
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_papers }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_political_power = 40
		WKR_ledger_legitimacy_up_2 = yes
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_papers }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8.c
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_false_trail }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_war_support = 0.01
		WKR_ledger_trust_down_3 = yes
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_false_trail }
	}
}

# The triage board divides power, bandages and wagons. Fired by
# WKR_ledger_triage_board and by the repeatable WKR_ledger_convene_triage.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.9
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.9.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.9.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_factory_workshop
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.9.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_manpower < 5000
			}
		}
		add_manpower = 2500
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_5 = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.9.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 35
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 3000 }
			}
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 2000
			producer = WKR
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			amount = 40
			producer = WKR
		}
		WKR_ledger_trust_down_5 = yes
	}
	# A split by norm works only where the districts trust the figures.
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.9.c
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { WKR_ledger_trust < 50 } } }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
		add_political_power = -15
		add_manpower = 1200
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 900
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}
