# --- B2 executive army events (ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.30-.37) ---

# Count Every Casualty: what the executive does with the first full casualty ledger.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.30
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.30.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.30.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_ledger_handover
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.30.a
		add_stability = 0.03
		add_war_support = -0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = {
				id = WKR_bop
				value = 0.05
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.30.b
		add_political_power = 35
		army_experience = 10
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.30.c
		add_manpower = 1500
		add_political_power = -15
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# Standard Battle Norms: which of three norms becomes the army's main table.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.31
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.31.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.31.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_headquarters
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.31.a
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = WKR_measure_density_norm
			days = 60
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 35 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.31.b
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = artillery_equipment_1
			amount = 24
			producer = WKR
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment_1
			amount = 30
			producer = WKR
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 35 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.31.c
		army_experience = 25
		add_command_power = 10
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# Greben's Firing Tables: the test at the ranging field.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.32
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.32.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.32.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_field_switchboard
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.32.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_equipment = { artillery_equipment < 18 } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = artillery_equipment
			amount = -18
		}
		army_experience = 30
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.32.b
		add_political_power = 30
		army_experience = 10
		ai_chance = { base = 35 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.32.c
		add_war_support = 0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = {
				id = WKR_bop
				value = 0.03
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
	}
}

# Foreign Licences: how the republic pays for the first licence.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.33
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.33.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.33.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.33.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { ADISCORD_economy_treasury < 100 } }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_measure_treasury_minus_100_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { WKR_measure_treasury_cost = 100 }
			WKR_measure_spend_treasury = yes
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment_1
			amount = 80
			producer = WKR
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.33.b
		add_political_power = -40
		add_stability = -0.02
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = ADISCORD_armored_carrier_2163
			amount = 30
			producer = WKR
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.33.c
		add_political_power = 20
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

				value = -0.03

		}

# Each candidate changes the regency aggregate; the previews match VAD_rgm_p1_* deltas.

	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.4
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.4.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.4.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_planning
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { VAD_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.4.a
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = VAD_rgm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = VAD_rgm_p1_rogov_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = VAD_rgm_army_org_factor value = 0.03 }
			add_to_variable = { var = VAD_rgm_army_org_regain value = 0.03 }
			VAD_rgm_refresh = yes
		}
		add_power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value = -0.05 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.4.b
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = VAD_rgm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = VAD_rgm_p1_vedenin_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = VAD_rgm_experience_gain_army_factor value = 0.10 }
			VAD_rgm_refresh = yes
		}
		add_doctrine_cost_reduction = {
			name = VAD_regent_vedenin_doctrine
			cost_reduction = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = land_doctrine
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.4.c
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = VAD_rgm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = VAD_rgm_p1_frolov_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = VAD_rgm_supply_consumption_factor value = -0.05 }
			VAD_rgm_refresh = yes
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 150 producer = VAD }
	}
}

# --- College of the general staff (collegial course) ---

# Fired by VAD_regent_put_the_operation_to_vote; repeatable with the decision.
# A binding majority adds stability to the voted directive; the veto opens the chairman's plan.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.10
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.10.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.10.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { VAD_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.10.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 surrender_progress > 0.20 }
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_regent_directive_offensive days = 45 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VAD_regent_bound_by_the_majority }
			add_stability = 0.01
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.10.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { factor = 2 surrender_progress > 0.20 }
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_regent_directive_defence days = 45 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VAD_regent_bound_by_the_majority }
			add_stability = 0.01
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.10.c
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_regent_directive_reorganisation days = 45 }
		army_experience = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VAD_regent_bound_by_the_majority }
			add_stability = 0.01
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.10.veto
		trigger = { has_completed_focus = VAD_regent_the_chairmans_veto }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_regent_directive_chairman days = 45 }
		add_stability = -0.02
	}
}

# --- W4: Worker story arcs (ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.100-139) ---
# Chain A, the scar of the Tower (100-112): Worker's wounds and the inquiry
# into the explosion. Worker's own scenes (100-107) need him with the
# loyalists. Choices: strain variable, suspect and seal flags, evidence.
# Chain B, commissars and poets (113-123): Irma Ladova, commissar Ilya Somov,
# Kravets and Velekhov. Choices: Ladova and Somov flags, the signed list.
# Chain C, the voice of the communities (124-133): kept promises against
# deferred debts, counted by the community congress.
# Block scenes (134-139) follow the railway, market and Vorkensberg choices.

# --- B4 executive story (ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.100-.139) ---

# The councils' newspaper prints line 4127. A buried or bought correction leaks
# as a cover-up; an open inquiry can be published in full.

	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_buried }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103.buried.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_bought }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103.bought.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_buried }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_bought }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103.d
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		WKR_administration_active = yes
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_method
			has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_scapegoat
			has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_relief
			has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_buried
			has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_bought
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103.a
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		add_political_power = -10
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_audit_hearing_tt
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.104 days = 7 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_buried
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 25
		add_stability = -0.02
		WKR_ledger_trust_down_8 = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_censored
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.106 days = 30 }
		}
	}
	# Only an inquiry that was actually held can be printed.
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.103.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_buried }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_bought }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		add_war_support = -0.02

		WKR_ledger_legitimacy_up_2 = yes
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.105 days = 14 } }
	}
}

# Kairholm answers Remmel. A settled strike makes the general terms cheaper; a
# crushed one makes the hard line pay in war support. Clears the strike flags.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.116
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.116.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_crushed }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.116.angry.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_crushed } }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.116.d
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_rally2
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		WKR_administration_active = yes
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_settled
			has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_crushed
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.116.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 35
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_settled
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_settled }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = -30
		}
		add_stability = 0.02
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_4 = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = WKR_audit_councils_heard
			WKR_audit_clear_strike_path = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.117 days = 20 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.116.b
		ai_chance = { base = 35 }
		add_political_power = -10
		add_stability = 0.01
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			amount = 40
			producer = WKR
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			WKR_audit_clear_strike_path = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.117 days = 20 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.116.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_crushed
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 20
		add_stability = -0.03
		add_war_support = 0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_crushed }
			add_war_support = 0.01
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = WKR_audit_councils_defied
			WKR_audit_clear_strike_path = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.117 days = 20 }
		}
	}
}

	}

		trigger = {

			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_councils_charter }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.123.d
	}

			base = 40

				factor = 1.5
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_councils_veto
			}
		}

				remove_idea = WKR_lrm_dummy_idea

		}

			clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_councils_veto

		}
	}

				factor = 1.5
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_councils_charter
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 40
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = WKR_lrm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = WKR_lrm_b4_norm_executive_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_lrm_industrial_capacity_factory value = 0.03 }

# Finale of chain C (WKR_audit_the_man_of_numbers). The chosen way of counting
# becomes a permanent term of the executive's aggregate; the remembered family,
# health and village flags are settled and cleared by every option.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.134
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.134.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_home }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.134.home.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_home }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_lost
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_renounced
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_refused
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.134.alone.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_home }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_lost }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_renounced }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_refused }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.134.d
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_administrative_office
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# P4 story arcs of Petrichev's branch (events .100-.135).
# A. "A uniform without a crown" (.100-.111): VAD_crownless_crown_pull counts how
#    far the old court has drawn Petrichev towards a crown; event .109 turns it
#    into VAD_crownless_crown_refused or VAD_crownless_crown_as_banner.
# B. "The general and his staff" (.112-.123): VAD_crownless_staff_lean (negative
#    favours Chernov, positive Vedenin) and VAD_crownless_staff_feud decide who
#    leads the assault at event .117.
# C. "The garrison rear" (.124-.135): VAD_crownless_rear_grudge counts the
#    grievances of Vereskovaya Pad; event .131 leaves VAD_crownless_rear_won or
#    VAD_crownless_rear_lost.
# Each closing event clears the transient state of its chain; the result flags
# are read by the P4 focuses and epilogue events and cleared by
# VAD_crownless_clear_story when the administration ends.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

# A1. Dispatched by VAD_regent_publish_the_service_record.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.100
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.100.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.100.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_administrative_office
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { VAD_administration_active = yes }

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { VAD_crownless_crown_pull = 0 }
	}

	option = {

		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
		add_war_support = 0.02
		army_experience = 10

	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.100.b
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		add_political_power = 40
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_regent_legitimacy_plus_2_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_crownless_pull_up_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.02 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			VAD_crownless_crown_pull_up = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.101 days = 30 }
		}
	}

		add_power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value = -0.03 }

	}

# A10. Closing event of the crown chain: sets one result flag and clears the rest.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { VAD_crownless_crown_pull < 3 } }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { VAD_crownless_crown_pull < 3 } } }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109.dh
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_headquarters
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { VAD_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109.a
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
		add_stability = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_regent_legitimacy_plus_5_tt
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAD_crownless_vow_sworn }
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_crownless_vow_kept_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAD_crownless_court_debt }
			add_political_power = -50
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_crownless_debt_withdrawn_tt
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_crownless_crown_refused_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.05 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = VAD_crownless_vow_sworn }
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta value = 0.02 }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			set_country_flag = VAD_crownless_crown_refused
			VAD_crownless_close_crown_chain = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109.b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { VAD_crownless_crown_pull > 2 }
			has_country_flag = VAD_crownless_regalia_held
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		add_war_support = 0.03
		add_command_power = 20
		add_stability = -0.04
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_regent_legitimacy_minus_2_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_crownless_crown_as_banner_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.02 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			set_country_flag = VAD_crownless_crown_as_banner
			VAD_crownless_close_crown_chain = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109.c
		trigger = { has_country_flag = VAD_crownless_genealogy_kept }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		add_political_power = -25
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_regent_legitimacy_plus_4_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_crownless_crown_refused_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.04 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			set_country_flag = VAD_crownless_crown_refused
			VAD_crownless_close_crown_chain = yes
		}

	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.100
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.100.t
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.100.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_railway_signal_box
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		TVA_administration_active = yes
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_open }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.100.a
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		add_command_power = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_protocol_single_operator }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_protocol_detachment_plus5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { TVA_protocol_detachment_delta = 5 }
				TVA_protocol_add_detachment = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_open
			set_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_ranked
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.101 days = 25 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.100.b
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		add_political_power = -20
		add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = 0.03 }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_open
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.101 days = 25 }
		}
	}
}

# --- W5: the decisive days (ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.140-167) ---
# The climax of the central war for Nikita Worcker's republic. The eve
# (140-145) runs on delayed follow-ups from the tunnels under Vorkensberg and
# reads who holds the Northern and the Central district; the city scenes
# (146-148, 154) follow the existing Vorkensberg focuses; the people scenes
# (149-166) follow the W5 focuses. Worker's own scenes (143, 149-152) need him
# with the loyalists. The speech (159) and the decisive days (163) close the
# line together in 167. State: WKR_last_exposure and WKR_last_* flags.

# Hook: WKR_front_rising_of_the_districts, 3 days. Kordov's earlier answer

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.146
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.146.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_waits }

	}
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.146.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_factory_workshop
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.146.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_manpower < 1000 } }
		add_manpower = -1000
		add_stability = 0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_waits }
			add_manpower = 2000
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_3_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_last_kordov_fate_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.03 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			clr_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_waits
			clr_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_out
			set_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_alive
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.146.b
		army_experience = 20
		add_war_support = 0.02
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_last_kordov_fate_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_waits
			clr_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_out
			set_country_flag = WKR_last_kordov_fell
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.146.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 800 } }
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = -800
		}
		add_command_power = 10
		if = {

		}

			base = 30
		}
	}

		add_manpower = 2000
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_war_support = -0.01
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_last_speech_promise_tt

# WKR_last_the_decisive_days, 3 days. The leader's crisis at the moment of
# victory or defeat. It foreshadows the postwar mandate and decides nothing
# about it: the unification and settlement code keep that decision.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { WKR_siege_chairman_present = no }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.d_committee
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { WKR_last_republic_bleeding = yes }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.d_defeat
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { WKR_last_victory_near = yes }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.d_victory
	}
	desc = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_planning
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.a
		add_political_power = -30
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_last_speech_siege_goes_on }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_2_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.02 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.05 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = WKR_last_mandate_returned
			WKR_last_mark_crisis_passed = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.166 days = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.b
		add_political_power = 50
		add_war_support = 0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -0.10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_last_speech_powers_end }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_legitimacy_minus_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.05 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_minus_2_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.02 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = WKR_last_powers_kept
			WKR_last_mark_crisis_passed = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.166 days = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.c
		trigger = {
			WKR_siege_chairman_present = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { WKR_last_exposure > 1 }
				has_country_flag = WKR_last_chairman_wounded
			}
		}
		WKR_last_ease_exposure = yes
		add_stability = 0.03
		add_command_power = 20
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.03 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			WKR_last_mark_crisis_passed = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.166 days = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.163.e
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_last_speech_amnesty }
		add_manpower = 3000
		add_stability = 0.02
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_2_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.02 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			WKR_last_mark_crisis_passed = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.166 days = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
	}

# --- B5: the decisive days ---
# Story line of Anton Bagley's executive at the climax of the central war.
# Thread A (.140-.143): the councils' last word. Thread B (.144-.150): the
# capital in the ledger. Thread C (.151-.157): the storm and its prisoners.
# Climax (.158-.161), the chairman's fate (.162-.165), epilogues and the final
# ledger (.166-.171), and the recorder of the earlier chains (.172). Every flag
# of the story is cleared by WKR_tally_close_story_chain at event .171 or by
# WKR_tally_clear_story_state when the administration closes.

# Thread A, seats path: Kober's three demands. The answer sets the councils'
# mood read by event .142 ten days later.

# Thread A: the night before the storm. Consumes the councils' mood and sets

			has_country_flag = WKR_tally_radicals_accepted
			has_country_flag = WKR_tally_radicals_bargained
			has_country_flag = WKR_tally_radicals_refused
		}
	}

		WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes

			set_country_flag = WKR_tally_councils_with_us
		}
	}
	option = {

		}

# Climax dispatcher, fired by the first of the three late rejoins (the last line
# of the war, the prisoner from the top, the last congress). The capital front
# decides which night Bagley lives through.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.158
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { WKR_front_holds_the_capital_core = yes }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.159 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { controls_state = 32 }
					surrender_progress > 0.25
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.160 }
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.161 }
		}
	}
}

# Finale: the war's final ledger. Each answer adds a permanent term to the
# executive's aggregate and closes the story through WKR_tally_close_story_chain.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_end_defeat }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171.defeat.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_end_stalemate }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171.stalemate.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_end_defeat }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_end_stalemate }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171.d
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_ledger_handover
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { WKR_administration_active = yes }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171.a
		ai_chance = { base = 35 }
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_8 = yes
		WKR_ledger_legitimacy_up_4 = yes
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = WKR_lrm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = WKR_lrm_b5_ledger_published_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_lrm_stability_factor value = 0.03 }
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_lrm_war_support_factor value = -0.02 }
			WKR_lrm_refresh = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { WKR_tally_city_debt > 2 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_tally_hunger_in_the_ledger_tt
			add_stability = -0.02
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_estimate_secret }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_tally_secret_estimate_published_tt
			add_war_support = -0.02
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_end_victory }
			WKR_ledger_legitimacy_up_2 = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = { WKR_tally_close_story_chain = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171.b
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes
		add_stability = 0.01
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = WKR_lrm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = WKR_lrm_b5_ledger_numbers_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_lrm_political_power_gain value = 0.05 }
			WKR_lrm_refresh = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_chairman_closed }
			WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = { WKR_tally_close_story_chain = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.171.c
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		add_political_power = 50
		WKR_ledger_trust_down_6 = yes
		add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -0.05 }
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = WKR_lrm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = WKR_lrm_b5_ledger_sealed_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_lrm_political_power_gain value = 0.08 }
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_lrm_stability_factor value = -0.03 }
			WKR_lrm_refresh = yes
		}

	}
	option = {

		ai_chance = { base = 20 }

# --- P5: the decisive days (ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.140-.170) ---
# Spine: eve .140 -> letters .141, the belfry .142, the generals .143, the colours
# .144, H-hour .145 -> bounded assault check .146 -> the Central District falls
# (.147) or the assault stalls (.148) and the siege drags (.149). The fall opens the
# city line (.150-.152), the captured (.153-.154) and the bounded crisis check .159
# -> victory .160 or defeat .161. The crisis outcome dispatches the epilogues
# .164-.168 and .170 and the closing event .169, which clears the story state.
# Entries: hooks on the storm, ring and course capstones, and VAD_standard_the_corps_under_the_walls.

# D6. H-hour. Reads the first objective (.140), the letters (.141), the hospital
# trains and the prisoners' march companies; starts the bounded assault check.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_letters_open }
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.dl
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_letters_open }
			has_country_flag = VAD_standard_aim_central
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_letters_open }
			has_country_flag = VAD_standard_aim_works
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.dw
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_letters_open }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_aim_central }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_aim_works }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.dr
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_battle
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		VAD_administration_active = yes
		VAD_realm_showdown_live_war = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { VAD_standard_assault_checks = 0 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.146 days = 21 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_letters_open }
			NOT = { has_manpower < 3000 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 45 }
		add_manpower = -3000
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_standard_decisive_assault days = 30 }
		add_war_support = 0.01
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VAD_standard_hospital_trains }
			add_manpower = 1000
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.e
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = VAD_standard_letters_open
			NOT = { has_manpower < 4500 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 45 }
		add_manpower = -4500
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_standard_decisive_assault days = 30 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VAD_standard_hospital_trains }
			add_manpower = 1000
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.b
		trigger = { NOT = { has_equipment = { artillery_equipment < 40 } } }
		ai_chance = { base = 35 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = -40 }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_standard_rolling_barrage days = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.145.f
		trigger = { has_completed_focus = VAD_standard_march_companies_of_prisoners }

# D7. Hidden. Checks the Central District three weeks after H-hour and then at
# most twice more, thirty days apart; the third failure declares the siege.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.146
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { VAD_administration_active = yes }

	immediate = {
		add_to_variable = { var = VAD_standard_assault_checks value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { controls_state = 32 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.147 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VAD_standard_assault_checks < 3 } }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.148 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.146 days = 30 }
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.149 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.159 days = 14 }
		}
	}
}

# D20. Hidden. The leader's crisis: victory while the Central District is held,
# defeat once the surrender progress passes 35%, otherwise at most two more
# checks thirty days apart before the siege is declared. Runs its outcome once.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.159
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { VAD_administration_active = yes }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_standard_crisis_done } }
			if = {
				limit = { controls_state = 32 }
				set_country_flag = VAD_standard_crisis_done
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.160 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { surrender_progress > 0.35 }
				set_country_flag = VAD_standard_crisis_done
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.161 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { VAD_standard_crisis_checks < 2 } }
				add_to_variable = { var = VAD_standard_crisis_checks value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.159 days = 30 }
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = VAD_standard_crisis_done
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.149 }
				VAD_standard_dispatch_epilogues = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# D21. Victory: the crown in the vault of the ministry of the court. No course
# crowns anyone during the civil war; the options differ in what the army sees.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.160
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.160.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {

			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_crownless_crown_as_banner }

# Chain end. Every option clears the endgame flags and any earlier chain flags.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178
	title = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178.d
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_endgame_hour_ring }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_endgame_hour_long_war }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178.ring.d
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = TVA_endgame_hour_ring
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178.long.d
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = TVA_endgame_hour_long_war
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_registry_office_keys
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		TVA_administration_active = yes
		has_country_flag = TVA_endgame_epilogues_open
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178.a
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = 0.08 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_protocol_legitimacy_plus3_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.03 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			TVA_endgame_clear_story = yes
			TVA_proof_close_all_chains = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178.b
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = -0.08 }
		add_political_power = 40
		hidden_effect = {
			TVA_endgame_clear_story = yes
			TVA_proof_close_all_chains = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178.c
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
		add_command_power = 20
		add_war_support = 0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_protocol_single_operator }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_protocol_detachment_plus10_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { TVA_protocol_detachment_delta = 10 }
				TVA_protocol_add_detachment = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			TVA_endgame_clear_story = yes
			TVA_proof_close_all_chains = yes
		}
	}
}
